187:                 <th>Test</th>
188:                 <th class="query-col">Description</th>
189: """)
190: 
191:     # Add column headers for train queries
192:     for qinfo in train_queries:
193:         polarity = "positive-col" if qinfo["should_trigger"] else "negative-col"
194:         html_parts.append(f'                <th class="{polarity}">{html.escape(qinfo["query"])}</th>\n')
195: 
196:     # Add column headers for test queries (different color)
197:     for qinfo in test_queries:
198:         polarity = "positive-col" if qinfo["should_trigger"] else "negative-col"
199:         html_parts.append(f'                <th class="test-col {polarity}">{html.escape(qinfo["query"])}</th>\n')
200: 
201:     html_parts.append("""            </tr>
202:         </thead>
203:         <tbody>
204: """)
205: 
206:     # Find best iteration for highlighting
207:     if test_queries:
208:         best_iter = max(history, key=lambda h: h.get("test_passed") or 0).get("iteration")
209:     else:
210:         best_iter = max(history, key=lambda h: h.get("train_passed", h.get("passed", 0))).get("iteration")
211: 
212:     # Add rows for each iteration
213:     for h in history:
214:         iteration = h.get("iteration", "?")
215:         train_passed = h.get("train_passed", h.get("passed", 0))
216:         train_total = h.get("train_total", h.get("total", 0))
217:         test_passed = h.get("test_passed")
218:         test_total = h.get("test_total")
219:         description = h.get("description", "")
220:         train_results = h.get("train_results", h.get("results", []))
221:         test_results = h.get("test_results", [])
222:         if test_results is None:
223:             test_results = []
224: 
225:         # Create lookups for results by query
226:         train_by_query = {r["query"]: r for r in train_results}
227:         test_by_query = {r["query"]: r for r in test_results} if test_results else {}
228: 
229:         # Compute aggregate correct/total runs across all retries
230:         def aggregate_runs(results: list[dict]) -> tuple[int, int]:
231:             correct = 0
232:             total = 0
233:             for r in results:
234:                 runs = r.get("runs", 0)
235:                 triggers = r.get("triggers", 0)
236:                 total += runs
237:                 if r.get("should_trigger", True):
238:                     correct += triggers
239:                 else:
240:                     correct += runs - triggers
241:             return correct, total
242: 
243:         train_correct, train_runs = aggregate_runs(train_results)
244:         test_correct, test_runs = aggregate_runs(test_results)
245: 
246:         # Determine score classes
247:         def score_class(correct: int, total: int) -> str:
248:             if total == 0:
249:                 return "score-na"
250:             if total > 0:
251:                 ratio = correct / total
252:                 if ratio >= 0.8:
253:                     return "score-good"
254:                 elif ratio >= 0.5:
255:                     return "score-ok"
256:             return "score-bad"
257: 
258:         train_class = score_class(train_correct, train_runs)
259:         test_class = score_class(test_correct, test_runs)
260:         train_score = f"{train_correct}/{train_runs}" if train_runs else "N/A"
261:         test_score = f"{test_correct}/{test_runs}" if test_runs else "N/A"
262: 
263:         row_class = "best-row" if iteration == best_iter else ""
264: 
265:         html_parts.append(f"""            <tr class="{row_class}">
266:                 <td>{iteration}</td>
267:                 <td><span class="score {train_class}">{train_score}</span></td>
268:                 <td><span class="score {test_class}">{test_score}</span></td>
269:                 <td class="description">{html.escape(description)}</td>
270: """)
271: 
272:         # Add result for each train query
273:         for qinfo in train_queries:
274:             r = train_by_query.get(qinfo["query"], {})
275:             did_pass = r.get("pass", False)
276:             triggers = r.get("triggers", 0)
277:             runs = r.get("runs", 0)
278: 
279:             icon = "✓" if did_pass else "✗"
280:             css_class = "pass" if did_pass else "fail"
281: 
282:             html_parts.append(f'                <td class="result {css_class}">{icon}<span class="rate">{triggers}/{runs}</span></td>\n')
283: 
284:         # Add result for each test query (with different background)
285:         for qinfo in test_queries:
286:             r = test_by_query.get(qinfo["query"], {})
287:             did_pass = r.get("pass", False)
288:             triggers = r.get("triggers", 0)
289:             runs = r.get("runs", 0)
290: 
291:             icon = "✓" if did_pass else "✗"
292:             css_class = "pass" if did_pass else "fail"
293: 
294:             html_parts.append(f'                <td class="result test-result {css_class}">{icon}<span class="rate">{triggers}/{runs}</span></td>\n')
295: 
296:         html_parts.append("            </tr>\n")
297: 
298:     html_parts.append("""        </tbody>
299:     </table>
300:     </div>
301: """)
302: 
303:     html_parts.append("""
304: </body>
305: </html>
306: """)
307: 
308:     return "".join(html_parts)
309: 
310: 
311: def main():
312:     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Generate HTML report from run_loop output")
313:     parser.add_argument("input", help="Path to JSON output from run_loop.py (or - for stdin)")
314:     parser.add_argument("-o", "--output", default=None, help="Output HTML file (default: stdout)")
315:     parser.add_argument("--skill-name", default="", help="Skill name to include in the report title")
316:     args = parser.parse_args()
317: 
318:     if args.input == "-":
319:         data = json.load(sys.stdin)
320:     else:
321:         data = json.loads(Path(args.input).read_text())
322: 
323:     html_output = generate_html(data, skill_name=args.skill_name)
324: 
325:     if args.output:
326:         Path(args.output).write_text(html_output)
327:         print(f"Report written to {args.output}", file=sys.stderr)
328:     else:
329:         print(html_output)
330: 
331: 
332: if __name__ == "__main__":
333:     main()

FILE skill-creator-advance/scripts/improve_description.py
1: #!/usr/bin/env python3
2: """Improve a skill description based on eval results.
3: 
4: Takes eval results (from run_eval.py) and generates an improved description
5: by calling `claude -p` as a subprocess (same auth pattern as run_eval.py —
6: uses the session's Claude Code auth, no separate ANTHROPIC_API_KEY needed).
7: """
8: 
9: import argparse
10: import json
11: import os
12: import re
13: import subprocess
14: import sys
15: from pathlib import Path
16: 
17: from scripts.quick_validate import description_violation
18: from scripts.utils import parse_skill_md
19: 
20: 
21: def _call_claude(prompt: str, model: str | None, timeout: int = 300) -> str:
22:     """Run `claude -p` with the prompt on stdin and return the text response.
23: 
24:     Prompt goes over stdin (not argv) because it embeds the full SKILL.md
25:     body and can easily exceed comfortable argv length.
26:     """
27:     cmd = ["claude", "-p", "--output-format", "text"]
28:     if model:
29:         cmd.extend(["--model", model])
30: 
31:     # Remove CLAUDECODE env var to allow nesting claude -p inside a
32:     # Claude Code session. The guard is for interactive terminal conflicts;
33:     # programmatic subprocess usage is safe. Same pattern as run_eval.py.
34:     env = {k: v for k, v in os.environ.items() if k != "CLAUDECODE"}
35: 
36:     result = subprocess.run(
37:         cmd,
38:         input=prompt,
39:         capture_output=True,
40:         text=True,
41:         env=env,
42:         timeout=timeout,
43:     )
44:     if result.returncode != 0:
45:         raise RuntimeError(
46:             f"claude -p exited {result.returncode}\nstderr: {result.stderr}"
47:         )
48:     return result.stdout
49: 
50: 
51: def improve_description(
52:     skill_name: str,
53:     skill_content: str,
54:     current_description: str,
55:     eval_results: dict,
56:     history: list[dict],
57:     model: str,
58:     test_results: dict | None = None,
59:     log_dir: Path | None = None,
60:     iteration: int | None = None,
61: ) -> str:
62:     """Call Claude to improve the description based on eval results."""
63:     failed_triggers = [
64:         r for r in eval_results["results"]
65:         if r["should_trigger"] and not r["pass"]
66:     ]
67:     false_triggers = [
68:         r for r in eval_results["results"]
69:         if not r["should_trigger"] and not r["pass"]
70:     ]
71: 
72:     # Build scores summary
73:     train_score = f"{eval_results['summary']['passed']}/{eval_results['summary']['total']}"
74:     if test_results:
75:         test_score = f"{test_results['summary']['passed']}/{test_results['summary']['total']}"
76:         scores_summary = f"Train: {train_score}, Test: {test_score}"
77:     else:
78:         scores_summary = f"Train: {train_score}"
79: 
80:     authority = (Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "references/description-design.md").read_text()
81:     prompt = f"""You are optimizing a skill description for a Claude Code skill called "{skill_name}". Follow the current bundled description authority below, including neutral third-person voice, positive intent triggers, and its contextual length exceptions. Existing SKILL.md frontmatter/comments supply exception context; do not invent justification or firing evidence.
82: 
83: {authority}
84: 
85: A skill uses progressive disclosure: its name and description help select it, while its body supplies the workflow.
86: 
87: The description appears in Claude's "available_skills" list. When a user sends a query, Claude decides whether to invoke the skill based solely on the title and on this description. Your goal is to write a description that triggers for relevant queries, and doesn't trigger for irrelevant ones.
88: 
89: Here's the current description:
90: <current_description>
91: "{current_description}"
92: </current_description>
93: 
94: Current scores ({scores_summary}):
95: <scores_summary>
96: """
97:     if failed_triggers:
98:         prompt += "FAILED TO TRIGGER (should have triggered but didn't):\n"
99:         for r in failed_triggers:
100:             prompt += f'  - "{r["query"]}" (triggered {r["triggers"]}/{r["runs"]} times)\n'
101:         prompt += "\n"
102: 
103:     if false_triggers:
104:         prompt += "FALSE TRIGGERS (triggered but shouldn't have):\n"
105:         for r in false_triggers:
106:             prompt += f'  - "{r["query"]}" (triggered {r["triggers"]}/{r["runs"]} times)\n'
107:         prompt += "\n"
108: 
109:     if history:
110:         prompt += "PREVIOUS ATTEMPTS (do NOT repeat these — try something structurally different):\n\n"
111:         for h in history:
112:             train_s = f"{h.get('train_passed', h.get('passed', 0))}/{h.get('train_total', h.get('total', 0))}"
113:             test_s = f"{h.get('test_passed', '?')}/{h.get('test_total', '?')}" if h.get('test_passed') is not None else None
114:             score_str = f"train={train_s}" + (f", test={test_s}" if test_s else "")
115:             prompt += f'<attempt {score_str}>\n'
116:             prompt += f'Description: "{h["description"]}"\n'
117:             if "results" in h:
118:                 prompt += "Train results:\n"
119:                 for r in h["results"]:
120:                     status = "PASS" if r["pass"] else "FAIL"
121:                     prompt += f'  [{status}] "{r["query"][:80]}" (triggered {r["triggers"]}/{r["runs"]})\n'
122:             if h.get("note"):
123:                 prompt += f'Note: {h["note"]}\n'
124:             prompt += "</attempt>\n\n"
125: 
126:     prompt += f"""</scores_summary>
127: 
128: Skill content (for context on what the skill does):
129: <skill_content>
130: {skill_content}
131: </skill_content>
132: 
133: Based on the failures, write a new and improved description that is more likely to trigger correctly. When I say "based on the failures", it's a bit of a tricky line to walk because we don't want to overfit to the specific cases you're seeing. So what I DON'T want you to do is produce an ever-expanding list of specific queries that this skill should or shouldn't trigger for. Instead, try to generalize from the failures to broader categories of user intent and situations where this skill would be useful or not useful. The reason for this is twofold:
134: 
135: 1. Avoid overfitting
136: 2. The list might get loooong and it's injected into ALL queries and there might be a lot of skills, so we don't want to blow too much space on any given description.
137: 
138: Aim for 150 characters. The 250-character soft lint line allows longer normal descriptions only with the authority's colocated justification. Router/CONDITIONAL admission and 500-character exception bounds require the authority's firing evidence. The separate platform ceiling is 1024 characters.
139: 
140: Here are some tips that we've found to work well in writing these descriptions:
141: - Write neutral third-person text, for example "Converts CSV to JSONL when streaming output is needed."
142: - The skill description should focus on the user's intent, what they are trying to achieve, vs. the implementation details of how the skill works.
143: - The description competes with other skills for Claude's attention — make it distinctive and immediately recognizable.
144: - If you're getting lots of failures after repeated attempts, change things up. Try different sentence structures or wordings.
145: 
146: Try different structures while preserving these constraints. Only mechanically eligible candidates compete by score; the applying agent must still validate voice, trigger coverage and contextual justification.
147: 
148: Please respond with only the new description text in <new_description> tags, nothing else."""
149: 
150:     text = _call_claude(prompt, model)
151: 
152:     match = re.fullmatch(r"\s*<new_description>(.*?)</new_description>\s*", text, re.DOTALL)
153:     description = match.group(1).strip().strip('"') if match else text.strip().strip('"')
154: 
155:     transcript: dict = {
156:         "iteration": iteration,
157:         "prompt": prompt,
158:         "response": text,
159:         "parsed_description": description,
160:         "char_count": len(description),
161:         "over_limit": len(description) > 1024,
162:     }
163: 
164:     # One bounded repair for any mechanical violation, not just platform size.
165:     violation = description_violation(description, skill_content)
166:     if violation:
167:         shorten_prompt = (
168:             f"{prompt}\n\n"
169:             f"---\n\n"
170:             f"A previous description is ineligible: {violation}:\n\n"
171:             f'"{description}"\n\n'
172:             f"Rewrite it to satisfy the bundled authority and existing metadata while keeping the most "
173:             f"important trigger words and intent coverage. Respond with only "
174:             f"the new description in <new_description> tags."
175:         )
176:         shorten_text = _call_claude(shorten_prompt, model)
177:         match = re.fullmatch(r"\s*<new_description>(.*?)</new_description>\s*", shorten_text, re.DOTALL)
178:         shortened = match.group(1).strip().strip('"') if match else shorten_text.strip().strip('"')
179: 
180:         transcript["rewrite_prompt"] = shorten_prompt
181:         transcript["rewrite_response"] = shorten_text
182:         transcript["rewrite_description"] = shortened
183:         transcript["rewrite_char_count"] = len(shortened)
184:         description = shortened
185: 
186:     transcript["final_description"] = description
187: 
188:     if log_dir:
189:         log_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
190:         log_file = log_dir / f"improve_iter_{iteration or 'unknown'}.json"
191:         log_file.write_text(json.dumps(transcript, indent=2))
192: 
193:     violation = description_violation(description, skill_content)
194:     if violation:
195:         raise ValueError(f"Ineligible description: {violation}")
196:     return description
197: 
198: 
199: def main():
200:     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Improve a skill description based on eval results")
201:     parser.add_argument("--eval-results", required=True, help="Path to eval results JSON (from run_eval.py)")
202:     parser.add_argument("--skill-path", required=True, help="Path to skill directory")
203:     parser.add_argument("--history", default=None, help="Path to history JSON (previous attempts)")
204:     parser.add_argument("--model", required=True, help="Model for improvement")
205:     parser.add_argument("--verbose", action="store_true", help="Print thinking to stderr")
206:     args = parser.parse_args()
207: 
208:     skill_path = Path(args.skill_path)
209:     if not (skill_path / "SKILL.md").exists():
210:         print(f"Error: No SKILL.md found at {skill_path}", file=sys.stderr)
211:         sys.exit(1)
212: 
213:     eval_results = json.loads(Path(args.eval_results).read_text())
214:     history = []
215:     if args.history:
216:         history = json.loads(Path(args.history).read_text())
217: 
218:     name, _, content = parse_skill_md(skill_path)
219:     current_description = eval_results["description"]
220: 
221:     if args.verbose:
222:         print(f"Current: {current_description}", file=sys.stderr)
223:         print(f"Score: {eval_results['summary']['passed']}/{eval_results['summary']['total']}", file=sys.stderr)
224: 
225:     new_description = improve_description(
226:         skill_name=name,
227:         skill_content=content,
228:         current_description=current_description,
229:         eval_results=eval_results,
230:         history=history,
231:         model=args.model,
232:     )
233: 
234:     if args.verbose:
235:         print(f"Improved: {new_description}", file=sys.stderr)
236: 
237:     # Output as JSON with both the new description and updated history
238:     output = {
239:         "description": new_description,
240:         "history": history + [{
241:             "description": current_description,
242:             "passed": eval_results["summary"]["passed"],
243:             "failed": eval_results["summary"]["failed"],
244:             "total": eval_results["summary"]["total"],
245:             "results": eval_results["results"],
246:         }],
247:     }
248:     print(json.dumps(output, indent=2))
249: 
250: 
251: if __name__ == "__main__":
252:     main()

FILE skill-creator-advance/scripts/package_skill.py
1: #!/usr/bin/env python3
2: """
3: Skill Packager - Creates a distributable .skill file of a skill folder
4: 
5: Usage:
6:     python -m scripts.package_skill <path/to/skill-folder> [output-directory]
7: 
8: Example:
9:     python -m scripts.package_skill skills/public/my-skill
10:     python -m scripts.package_skill skills/public/my-skill ./dist
11: """
12: 
13: import fnmatch
14: import sys
15: import zipfile
16: from pathlib import Path
17: from scripts.quick_validate import validate_skill
18: 
19: # Patterns to exclude when packaging skills.
20: EXCLUDE_DIRS = {"__pycache__", "node_modules"}
21: EXCLUDE_GLOBS = {"*.pyc"}
22: EXCLUDE_FILES = {".DS_Store"}
23: # Directories excluded only at the skill root (not when nested deeper).
24: ROOT_EXCLUDE_DIRS = {"evals"}
25: 
26: 
27: def should_exclude(rel_path: Path) -> bool:
28:     """Check if a path should be excluded from packaging."""
29:     parts = rel_path.parts
30:     if any(part in EXCLUDE_DIRS for part in parts):
31:         return True
32:     # rel_path is relative to skill_path.parent, so parts[0] is the skill
33:     # folder name and parts[1] (if present) is the first subdir.
34:     if len(parts) > 1 and parts[1] in ROOT_EXCLUDE_DIRS:
35:         return True
36:     name = rel_path.name
37:     if name in EXCLUDE_FILES:
38:         return True
39:     return any(fnmatch.fnmatch(name, pat) for pat in EXCLUDE_GLOBS)
40: 
41: 
42: def package_skill(skill_path, output_dir=None):
43:     """
44:     Package a skill folder into a .skill file.
45: 
46:     Args:
47:         skill_path: Path to the skill folder
48:         output_dir: Optional output directory for the .skill file (defaults to current directory)
49: 
50:     Returns:
51:         Path to the created .skill file, or None if error
52:     """
53:     skill_path = Path(skill_path).resolve()
54: 
55:     # Validate skill folder exists
56:     if not skill_path.exists():
57:         print(f"❌ Error: Skill folder not found: {skill_path}")
58:         return None
59: 
60:     if not skill_path.is_dir():
61:         print(f"❌ Error: Path is not a directory: {skill_path}")
62:         return None
63: 
64:     # Validate SKILL.md exists
65:     skill_md = skill_path / "SKILL.md"
66:     if not skill_md.exists():
67:         print(f"❌ Error: SKILL.md not found in {skill_path}")
68:         return None
69: 
70:     # Run validation before packaging
71:     print("🔍 Validating skill...")
72:     valid, message = validate_skill(skill_path)
73:     if not valid:
74:         print(f"❌ Validation failed: {message}")
75:         print("   Please fix the validation errors before packaging.")
76:         return None
77:     print(f"✅ {message}\n")
78: 
79:     # Determine output location
80:     skill_name = skill_path.name
81:     if output_dir:
82:         output_path = Path(output_dir).resolve()
83:         output_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
84:     else:
85:         output_path = Path.cwd()
86: 
87:     skill_filename = output_path / f"{skill_name}.skill"
88: 
89:     # Create the .skill file (zip format)
90:     try:
91:         with zipfile.ZipFile(skill_filename, 'w', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zipf:
92:             # Walk through the skill directory, excluding build artifacts
93:             for file_path in skill_path.rglob('*'):
94:                 if not file_path.is_file():
95:                     continue
96:                 arcname = file_path.relative_to(skill_path.parent)
97:                 if should_exclude(arcname):
98:                     print(f"  Skipped: {arcname}")
99:                     continue
100:                 zipf.write(file_path, arcname)
101:                 print(f"  Added: {arcname}")
102: 
103:         print(f"\n✅ Successfully packaged skill to: {skill_filename}")
104:         return skill_filename
105: 
106:     except Exception as e:
107:         print(f"❌ Error creating .skill file: {e}")
108:         return None
109: 
110: 
111: def main():
112:     if len(sys.argv) < 2:
113:         print("Usage: python -m scripts.package_skill <path/to/skill-folder> [output-directory]")
114:         print("\nExample:")
115:         print("  python -m scripts.package_skill skills/public/my-skill")
116:         print("  python -m scripts.package_skill skills/public/my-skill ./dist")
117:         sys.exit(1)
118: 
119:     skill_path = sys.argv[1]
120:     output_dir = sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else None
121: 
122:     print(f"📦 Packaging skill: {skill_path}")
123:     if output_dir:
124:         print(f"   Output directory: {output_dir}")
125:     print()
126: 
127:     result = package_skill(skill_path, output_dir)
128: 
129:     if result:
130:         sys.exit(0)
131:     else:
132:         sys.exit(1)
133: 
134: 
135: if __name__ == "__main__":
136:     main()

FILE skill-creator-advance/scripts/quick_validate.py
1: #!/usr/bin/env python3
2: """
3: Quick validation script for skills - minimal version
4: """
5: 
6: import sys
7: import os
8: import re
9: import yaml
10: from pathlib import Path
11: 
12: 
13: def description_violation(description: str, skill_content: str) -> str | None:
14:     """Check mechanical eligibility; contextual justification still needs review."""
15:     if not description.strip() or "<" in description or ">" in description:
16:         return "description must be nonempty text without XML/angle brackets"
17:     if len(description) > 1024:
18:         return "description exceeds the platform ceiling of 1024 characters"
19:     match = re.match(r"\A---\n(.*?)\n---(?:\n|$)", skill_content, re.DOTALL)
20:     if not match:
21:         return "description context has malformed YAML frontmatter"
22:     try:
23:         metadata = yaml.safe_load(match.group(1))
24:     except yaml.YAMLError:
25:         return "description context has malformed YAML frontmatter"
26:     if not isinstance(metadata, dict) or not isinstance(metadata.get("description"), str):
27:         return "description context needs a YAML description string"
28:     # Comments do not count toward rendered length. Only the comment block
29:     # directly above the parsed description field can justify a long value.
30:     lines = match.group(1).splitlines()
31:     comments = []
32:     for index, line in enumerate(lines):
33:         if line.startswith("description:"):
34:             for previous in reversed(lines[:index]):
35:                 if not previous.startswith("#"):
36:                     break
37:                 comments.insert(0, previous[1:].strip())
38:             break
39:     justification = " ".join(comments)
40:     firing_note = re.search(r"\bFiring evidence:\s*(\S.*)", justification, re.IGNORECASE)
41:     # Domain nouns in a KEEP explanation do not declare the skill's role.
42:     role_declaration = any(re.match(
43:         r"^(?:(?:skill\s+)?role:\s*(?:router|CONDITIONAL)\b|"
44:         r"(?:router|CONDITIONAL)(?:\s+skill\b|\s*[:.;]|\s*$))",
45:         comment, re.IGNORECASE,
46:     ) for comment in comments)
47:     declared_exception = firing_note or role_declaration or re.search(r"Firing evidence:", justification, re.IGNORECASE)
48:     if declared_exception and len(description) > 250:
49:         if not firing_note:
50:             return "description router/CONDITIONAL exception needs a firing-evidence note"
51:         if len(description) > 500:
52:             return "description exceeds the admitted router/CONDITIONAL bound of 500 characters"
53:     if len(description) > 250 and not justification:
54:         return "description over 250 characters needs a colocated YAML justification"
55:     return None
56: 
57: 
58: def validate_skill(skill_path):
59:     """Basic validation of a skill"""
60:     skill_path = Path(skill_path)
61: 
62:     # Check SKILL.md exists
63:     skill_md = skill_path / 'SKILL.md'
64:     if not skill_md.exists():
65:         return False, "SKILL.md not found"
66: 
67:     # Read and validate frontmatter
68:     content = skill_md.read_text()
69:     if not content.startswith('---'):
70:         return False, "No YAML frontmatter found"
71: 
72:     # Extract frontmatter
73:     match = re.match(r'^---\n(.*?)\n---', content, re.DOTALL)
74:     if not match:
75:         return False, "Invalid frontmatter format"
76: 
77:     frontmatter_text = match.group(1)
78: 
79:     # Parse YAML frontmatter
80:     try:
81:         frontmatter = yaml.safe_load(frontmatter_text)
82:         if not isinstance(frontmatter, dict):
83:             return False, "Frontmatter must be a YAML dictionary"
84:     except yaml.YAMLError as e:
85:         return False, f"Invalid YAML in frontmatter: {e}"
86: 
87:     # Define allowed properties
88:     ALLOWED_PROPERTIES = {'name', 'description', 'license', 'allowed-tools', 'metadata', 'compatibility'}
89: 
90:     # Check for unexpected properties (excluding nested keys under metadata)
91:     unexpected_keys = set(frontmatter.keys()) - ALLOWED_PROPERTIES
92:     if unexpected_keys:
93:         return False, (
94:             f"Unexpected key(s) in SKILL.md frontmatter: {', '.join(sorted(unexpected_keys))}. "
95:             f"Allowed properties are: {', '.join(sorted(ALLOWED_PROPERTIES))}"
96:         )
97: 
98:     # Check required fields
99:     if 'name' not in frontmatter:
100:         return False, "Missing 'name' in frontmatter"
101:     if 'description' not in frontmatter:
102:         return False, "Missing 'description' in frontmatter"
103: 
104:     # Extract name for validation
105:     name = frontmatter.get('name', '')
106:     if not isinstance(name, str):
107:         return False, f"Name must be a string, got {type(name).__name__}"
108:     name = name.strip()
109:     if name:
110:         # Check naming convention (kebab-case: lowercase with hyphens)
111:         if not re.match(r'^[a-z0-9-]+$', name):
112:             return False, f"Name '{name}' should be kebab-case (lowercase letters, digits, and hyphens only)"
113:         if name.startswith('-') or name.endswith('-') or '--' in name:
114:             return False, f"Name '{name}' cannot start/end with hyphen or contain consecutive hyphens"
115:         # Check name length (max 64 characters per spec)
116:         if len(name) > 64:
117:             return False, f"Name is too long ({len(name)} characters). Maximum is 64 characters."
118: 
119:     # Extract and validate description
120:     description = frontmatter.get('description', '')
121:     if not isinstance(description, str):
122:         return False, f"Description must be a string, got {type(description).__name__}"
123:     description = description.strip()
124:     violation = description_violation(description, content)
125:     if violation:
126:         return False, violation
127: 
128:     # Validate compatibility field if present (optional)
129:     compatibility = frontmatter.get('compatibility', '')
130:     if compatibility:
131:         if not isinstance(compatibility, str):
132:             return False, f"Compatibility must be a string, got {type(compatibility).__name__}"
133:         if len(compatibility) > 500:
134:             return False, f"Compatibility is too long ({len(compatibility)} characters). Maximum is 500 characters."
135: 
136:     return True, "Skill is valid!"
137: 
138: if __name__ == "__main__":
139:     if len(sys.argv) != 2:
140:         print("Usage: python quick_validate.py <skill_directory>")
141:         sys.exit(1)
142:     
143:     valid, message = validate_skill(sys.argv[1])
144:     print(message)
145:     sys.exit(0 if valid else 1)

FILE skill-creator-advance/scripts/run_eval.py
1: #!/usr/bin/env python3
2: """Run trigger evaluation for a skill description.
3: 
4: Tests whether a skill's description causes Claude to trigger (read the skill)
5: for a set of queries. Outputs results as JSON.
6: """
7: 
8: import argparse
9: import json
10: import os
11: import select
12: import subprocess
13: import sys
14: import time
15: import uuid
16: from concurrent.futures import ProcessPoolExecutor, as_completed
17: from pathlib import Path
18: 
19: from scripts.utils import parse_skill_md
20: 
21: 
22: def find_project_root() -> Path:
23:     """Find the project root by walking up from cwd looking for .claude/.
24: 
25:     Mimics how Claude Code discovers its project root, so the command file
26:     we create ends up where claude -p will look for it.
27:     """
28:     current = Path.cwd()
29:     for parent in [current, *current.parents]:
30:         if (parent / ".claude").is_dir():
31:             return parent
32:     return current
33: 
34: 
35: def run_single_query(
36:     query: str,
37:     skill_name: str,
38:     skill_description: str,
39:     timeout: int,
40:     project_root: str,
41:     model: str | None = None,
42: ) -> bool:
43:     """Run a single query and return whether the skill was triggered.
44: 
45:     Creates a command file in .claude/commands/ so it appears in Claude's
46:     available_skills list, then runs `claude -p` with the raw query.
47:     Uses --include-partial-messages to detect triggering early from
48:     stream events (content_block_start) rather than waiting for the
49:     full assistant message, which only arrives after tool execution.
50:     """
51:     unique_id = uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]
52:     clean_name = f"{skill_name}-skill-{unique_id}"
53:     project_commands_dir = Path(project_root) / ".claude" / "commands"
54:     command_file = project_commands_dir / f"{clean_name}.md"
55: 
56:     try:
57:         project_commands_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
58:         # Use YAML block scalar to avoid breaking on quotes in description
59:         indented_desc = "\n  ".join(skill_description.split("\n"))
60:         command_content = (
61:             f"---\n"
62:             f"description: |\n"
63:             f"  {indented_desc}\n"
64:             f"---\n\n"
65:             f"# {skill_name}\n\n"
66:             f"This skill handles: {skill_description}\n"
67:         )
68:         command_file.write_text(command_content)
69: 
70:         cmd = [
71:             "claude",
72:             "-p", query,
73:             "--output-format", "stream-json",
74:             "--verbose",
75:             "--include-partial-messages",
76:         ]
77:         if model:
78:             cmd.extend(["--model", model])
79: 
80:         # Remove CLAUDECODE env var to allow nesting claude -p inside a
81:         # Claude Code session. The guard is for interactive terminal conflicts;
82:         # programmatic subprocess usage is safe.
83:         env = {k: v for k, v in os.environ.items() if k != "CLAUDECODE"}
84: 
85:         process = subprocess.Popen(
86:             cmd,
87:             stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
88:             stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
89:             cwd=project_root,
90:             env=env,
91:         )
92: 
93:         triggered = False
94:         start_time = time.time()
95:         buffer = ""
96:         # Track state for stream event detection
97:         pending_tool_name = None
98:         accumulated_json = ""
99: 
100:         try:
101:             while time.time() - start_time < timeout:
102:                 completed = process.poll() is not None
103:                 if completed:
104:                     remaining = process.stdout.read()
105:                     if remaining:
106:                         buffer += remaining.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
107:                     if process.returncode != 0:
108:                         raise RuntimeError(f"Query backend exited with status {process.returncode}")
109:                     # A completed process can leave its final event buffered,
110:                     # including a last line without a trailing newline.
111:                     buffer += "\n"
112:                 else:
113:                     ready, _, _ = select.select([process.stdout], [], [], 1.0)
114:                     if not ready:
115:                         continue
116: 
117:                     chunk = os.read(process.stdout.fileno(), 8192)
118:                     if not chunk:
119:                         # Stdout may close before poll observes process exit.
120:                         # Confirm status before parsing the unterminated tail.
121:                         try:
122:                             process.wait(timeout=max(0, timeout - (time.time() - start_time)))
123:                         except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as exc:
124:                             raise RuntimeError("Query backend timed out before a conclusive result") from exc
125:                         if process.returncode != 0:
126:                             raise RuntimeError(f"Query backend exited with status {process.returncode}")
127:                         completed = True
128:                         buffer += "\n"
129:                     else:
130:                         buffer += chunk.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
131: 
132:                 while "\n" in buffer:
133:                     line, buffer = buffer.split("\n", 1)
134:                     line = line.strip()
135:                     if not line:
136:                         continue
137: 
138:                     try:
139:                         event = json.loads(line)
140:                     except json.JSONDecodeError:
141:                         continue
142: 
143:                     # Early detection via stream events
144:                     if event.get("type") == "stream_event":
145:                         se = event.get("event", {})
146:                         se_type = se.get("type", "")
147: 
148:                         if se_type == "content_block_start":
149:                             cb = se.get("content_block", {})
150:                             if cb.get("type") == "tool_use":
151:                                 tool_name = cb.get("name", "")
152:                                 if tool_name in ("Skill", "Read"):
153:                                     pending_tool_name = tool_name
154:                                     accumulated_json = ""
155:                                 else:
156:                                     return False
157: 
158:                         elif se_type == "content_block_delta" and pending_tool_name:
159:                             delta = se.get("delta", {})
160:                             if delta.get("type") == "input_json_delta":
161:                                 accumulated_json += delta.get("partial_json", "")
162:                                 if clean_name in accumulated_json:
163:                                     return True
164: 
165:                         elif se_type in ("content_block_stop", "message_stop"):
166:                             if pending_tool_name:
167:                                 return clean_name in accumulated_json
168:                             if se_type == "message_stop":
169:                                 return False
170: 
171:                     # Fallback: full assistant message
172:                     elif event.get("type") == "assistant":
173:                         message = event.get("message", {})
174:                         for content_item in message.get("content", []):
175:                             if content_item.get("type") != "tool_use":
176:                                 continue
177:                             tool_name = content_item.get("name", "")
178:                             tool_input = content_item.get("input", {})
179:                             if tool_name == "Skill" and clean_name in tool_input.get("skill", ""):
180:                                 triggered = True
181:                             elif tool_name == "Read" and clean_name in tool_input.get("file_path", ""):
182:                                 triggered = True
183:                             return triggered
184: 
185:                     elif event.get("type") == "result":
186:                         # ResultMessage can report success for an interrupted,
187:                         # incomplete turn. Older CLI versions omit this field.
188:                         # https://code.claude.com/docs/en/agent-sdk/python#resultmessage
189:                         if event.get("terminal_reason") in ("aborted_streaming", "aborted_tools"):
190:                             raise RuntimeError("Query backend aborted before completing")
191:                         if event.get("is_error") or event.get("subtype") != "success":
192:                             raise RuntimeError("Query backend returned an unsuccessful result")
193:                         return triggered
194: 
195:                 if completed:
196:                     raise RuntimeError("Query backend ended without a conclusive result")
197:         finally:
198:             # Clean up process on any exit path (return, exception, timeout)
199:             if process.poll() is None:
200:                 process.kill()
201:                 process.wait()
202: 
203:         raise RuntimeError("Query backend timed out before a conclusive result")
204:     finally:
205:         if command_file.exists():
206:             command_file.unlink()
207: 
208: 
209: def run_eval(
210:     eval_set: list[dict],
211:     skill_name: str,
212:     description: str,
213:     num_workers: int,
214:     timeout: int,
215:     project_root: Path,
216:     runs_per_query: int = 1,
217:     trigger_threshold: float = 0.5,
218:     model: str | None = None,
219: ) -> dict:
220:     """Run the full eval set and return results."""
221:     results = []
222: 
223:     with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:
224:         future_to_info = {}
225:         for item in eval_set:
226:             for run_idx in range(runs_per_query):
227:                 future = executor.submit(
228:                     run_single_query,
229:                     item["query"],
230:                     skill_name,
231:                     description,
232:                     timeout,
233:                     str(project_root),
234:                     model,
235:                 )
236:                 future_to_info[future] = (item, run_idx)
237: 
238:         query_triggers: dict[str, list[bool]] = {}
239:         query_items: dict[str, dict] = {}
240:         for future in as_completed(future_to_info):
241:             item, _ = future_to_info[future]
242:             query = item["query"]
243:             query_items[query] = item
244:             if query not in query_triggers:
245:                 query_triggers[query] = []
246:             # A failed run is not a successful negative observation. Propagate
247:             # it before producing scores or allowing the optimizer to proceed.
248:             query_triggers[query].append(future.result())
249: 
250:     for query, triggers in query_triggers.items():
251:         item = query_items[query]
252:         trigger_rate = sum(triggers) / len(triggers)
253:         should_trigger = item["should_trigger"]
254:         if should_trigger:
255:             did_pass = trigger_rate >= trigger_threshold
256:         else:
257:             did_pass = trigger_rate < trigger_threshold
258:         results.append({
259:             "query": query,
260:             "should_trigger": should_trigger,
261:             "trigger_rate": trigger_rate,
262:             "triggers": sum(triggers),
263:             "runs": len(triggers),
264:             "pass": did_pass,
265:         })
266: 
267:     passed = sum(1 for r in results if r["pass"])
268:     total = len(results)
269: 
270:     return {
271:         "skill_name": skill_name,
272:         "description": description,
273:         "results": results,
274:         "summary": {
275:             "total": total,
276:             "passed": passed,
277:             "failed": total - passed,
278:         },
279:     }
280: 
281: 
282: def main():
283:     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Run trigger evaluation for a skill description")
284:     parser.add_argument("--eval-set", required=True, help="Path to eval set JSON file")
285:     parser.add_argument("--skill-path", required=True, help="Path to skill directory")
286:     parser.add_argument("--description", default=None, help="Override description to test")
287:     parser.add_argument("--num-workers", type=int, default=10, help="Number of parallel workers")
288:     parser.add_argument("--timeout", type=int, default=30, help="Timeout per query in seconds")
289:     parser.add_argument("--runs-per-query", type=int, default=3, help="Number of runs per query")
290:     parser.add_argument("--trigger-threshold", type=float, default=0.5, help="Trigger rate threshold")
291:     parser.add_argument("--model", default=None, help="Model to use for claude -p (default: user's configured model)")
292:     parser.add_argument("--verbose", action="store_true", help="Print progress to stderr")
293:     args = parser.parse_args()
294: 
295:     eval_set = json.loads(Path(args.eval_set).read_text())
296:     skill_path = Path(args.skill_path)
297: 
298:     if not (skill_path / "SKILL.md").exists():
299:         print(f"Error: No SKILL.md found at {skill_path}", file=sys.stderr)
300:         sys.exit(1)
301: 
302:     name, original_description, content = parse_skill_md(skill_path)
303:     description = args.description or original_description
304:     project_root = find_project_root()
305: 
306:     if args.verbose:
307:         print(f"Evaluating: {description}", file=sys.stderr)
308: 
309:     output = run_eval(
310:         eval_set=eval_set,
311:         skill_name=name,
312:         description=description,
313:         num_workers=args.num_workers,
314:         timeout=args.timeout,
315:         project_root=project_root,
316:         runs_per_query=args.runs_per_query,
317:         trigger_threshold=args.trigger_threshold,
318:         model=args.model,
319:     )
320: 
321:     if args.verbose:
322:         summary = output["summary"]
323:         print(f"Results: {summary['passed']}/{summary['total']} passed", file=sys.stderr)
324:         for r in output["results"]:
325:             status = "PASS" if r["pass"] else "FAIL"
326:             rate_str = f"{r['triggers']}/{r['runs']}"
327:             print(f"  [{status}] rate={rate_str} expected={r['should_trigger']}: {r['query'][:70]}", file=sys.stderr)
328: 
329:     print(json.dumps(output, indent=2))
330: 
331: 
332: if __name__ == "__main__":
333:     main()

FILE skill-creator-advance/scripts/run_loop.py
1: #!/usr/bin/env python3
2: """Run the eval + improve loop until all pass or max iterations reached.
3: 
4: Combines run_eval.py and improve_description.py in a loop, tracking history
5: and returning the best description found. Supports train/test split to prevent
6: overfitting.
7: """
8: 
9: import argparse
10: import json
11: import random
12: import sys
13: import tempfile
14: import time
15: import webbrowser
16: from pathlib import Path
17: 
18: from scripts.generate_report import generate_html
19: from scripts.improve_description import description_violation, improve_description
20: from scripts.run_eval import find_project_root, run_eval
21: from scripts.utils import parse_skill_md
22: 
23: 
24: def split_eval_set(eval_set: list[dict], holdout: float, seed: int = 42) -> tuple[list[dict], list[dict]]:
25:     """Split eval set into train and test sets, stratified by should_trigger."""
26:     random.seed(seed)
27: 
28:     # Separate by should_trigger
29:     trigger = [e for e in eval_set if e["should_trigger"]]
30:     no_trigger = [e for e in eval_set if not e["should_trigger"]]
31: 
32:     # Shuffle each group
33:     random.shuffle(trigger)
34:     random.shuffle(no_trigger)
35: 
36:     # Calculate split points
37:     n_trigger_test = max(1, int(len(trigger) * holdout))
38:     n_no_trigger_test = max(1, int(len(no_trigger) * holdout))
39: 
40:     # Split
41:     test_set = trigger[:n_trigger_test] + no_trigger[:n_no_trigger_test]
42:     train_set = trigger[n_trigger_test:] + no_trigger[n_no_trigger_test:]
43: 
44:     return train_set, test_set
45: 
46: 
47: def run_loop(
48:     eval_set: list[dict],
49:     skill_path: Path,
50:     description_override: str | None,
51:     num_workers: int,
52:     timeout: int,
53:     max_iterations: int,
54:     runs_per_query: int,
55:     trigger_threshold: float,
56:     holdout: float,
57:     model: str,
58:     verbose: bool,
59:     live_report_path: Path | None = None,
60:     log_dir: Path | None = None,
61: ) -> dict:
62:     """Run the eval + improvement loop."""
63:     project_root = find_project_root()
64:     name, original_description, content = parse_skill_md(skill_path)
65:     current_description = description_override or original_description
66: 
67:     # Split into train/test if holdout > 0
68:     if holdout > 0:
69:         train_set, test_set = split_eval_set(eval_set, holdout)
70:         if verbose:
71:             print(f"Split: {len(train_set)} train, {len(test_set)} test (holdout={holdout})", file=sys.stderr)
72:     else:
73:         train_set = eval_set
74:         test_set = []
75: 
76:     history = []
77:     exit_reason = "unknown"
78: 
79:     for iteration in range(1, max_iterations + 1):
80:         if verbose:
81:             print(f"\n{'='*60}", file=sys.stderr)
82:             print(f"Iteration {iteration}/{max_iterations}", file=sys.stderr)
83:             print(f"Description: {current_description}", file=sys.stderr)
84:             print(f"{'='*60}", file=sys.stderr)
85: 
86:         # Evaluate train + test together in one batch for parallelism
87:         all_queries = train_set + test_set
88:         t0 = time.time()
89:         all_results = run_eval(
90:             eval_set=all_queries,
91:             skill_name=name,
92:             description=current_description,
93:             num_workers=num_workers,
94:             timeout=timeout,
95:             project_root=project_root,
96:             runs_per_query=runs_per_query,
97:             trigger_threshold=trigger_threshold,
98:             model=model,
99:         )
100:         eval_elapsed = time.time() - t0
101: 
102:         # Split results back into train/test by matching queries
103:         train_queries_set = {q["query"] for q in train_set}
104:         train_result_list = [r for r in all_results["results"] if r["query"] in train_queries_set]
105:         test_result_list = [r for r in all_results["results"] if r["query"] not in train_queries_set]
106: 
107:         train_passed = sum(1 for r in train_result_list if r["pass"])
108:         train_total = len(train_result_list)
109:         train_summary = {"passed": train_passed, "failed": train_total - train_passed, "total": train_total}
110:         train_results = {"results": train_result_list, "summary": train_summary}
111: 
112:         if test_set:
113:             test_passed = sum(1 for r in test_result_list if r["pass"])
114:             test_total = len(test_result_list)
115:             test_summary = {"passed": test_passed, "failed": test_total - test_passed, "total": test_total}
116:             test_results = {"results": test_result_list, "summary": test_summary}
117:         else:
118:             test_results = None
119:             test_summary = None
120: 
121:         history.append({
122:             "iteration": iteration,
123:             "description": current_description,
124:             "eligible": description_violation(current_description, content) is None,
125:             "train_passed": train_summary["passed"],
126:             "train_failed": train_summary["failed"],
127:             "train_total": train_summary["total"],
128:             "train_results": train_results["results"],
129:             "test_passed": test_summary["passed"] if test_summary else None,
130:             "test_failed": test_summary["failed"] if test_summary else None,
131:             "test_total": test_summary["total"] if test_summary else None,
132:             "test_results": test_results["results"] if test_results else None,
133:             # For backward compat with report generator
134:             "passed": train_summary["passed"],
135:             "failed": train_summary["failed"],
136:             "total": train_summary["total"],
137:             "results": train_results["results"],
138:         })
139: 
140:         # Write live report if path provided
141:         if live_report_path:
142:             partial_output = {
143:                 "original_description": original_description,
144:                 "best_description": current_description if history[-1]["eligible"] else "",
145:                 "best_score": "in progress",
146:                 "iterations_run": len(history),
147:                 "holdout": holdout,
148:                 "train_size": len(train_set),
149:                 "test_size": len(test_set),
150:                 "history": history,
151:             }
152:             live_report_path.write_text(generate_html(partial_output, auto_refresh=True, skill_name=name))
153: 
154:         if verbose:
155:             def print_eval_stats(label, results, elapsed):
156:                 pos = [r for r in results if r["should_trigger"]]
157:                 neg = [r for r in results if not r["should_trigger"]]
158:                 tp = sum(r["triggers"] for r in pos)
159:                 pos_runs = sum(r["runs"] for r in pos)
160:                 fn = pos_runs - tp
161:                 fp = sum(r["triggers"] for r in neg)
162:                 neg_runs = sum(r["runs"] for r in neg)
163:                 tn = neg_runs - fp
164:                 total = tp + tn + fp + fn
165:                 precision = tp / (tp + fp) if (tp + fp) > 0 else 1.0
166:                 recall = tp / (tp + fn) if (tp + fn) > 0 else 1.0
167:                 accuracy = (tp + tn) / total if total > 0 else 0.0
168:                 print(f"{label}: {tp+tn}/{total} correct, precision={precision:.0%} recall={recall:.0%} accuracy={accuracy:.0%} ({elapsed:.1f}s)", file=sys.stderr)
169:                 for r in results:
170:                     status = "PASS" if r["pass"] else "FAIL"
171:                     rate_str = f"{r['triggers']}/{r['runs']}"
172:                     print(f"  [{status}] rate={rate_str} expected={r['should_trigger']}: {r['query'][:60]}", file=sys.stderr)
173: 
174:             print_eval_stats("Train", train_results["results"], eval_elapsed)
175:             if test_summary:
176:                 print_eval_stats("Test ", test_results["results"], 0)
177: 
178:         if train_summary["failed"] == 0 and history[-1]["eligible"]:
179:             exit_reason = f"all_passed (iteration {iteration})"
180:             if verbose:
181:                 print(f"\nAll train queries passed on iteration {iteration}!", file=sys.stderr)
182:             break
183: 
184:         if iteration == max_iterations:
185:             exit_reason = f"max_iterations ({max_iterations})"
186:             if verbose:
187:                 print(f"\nMax iterations reached ({max_iterations}).", file=sys.stderr)
188:             break
189: 
190:         # Improve the description based on train results
191:         if verbose:
192:             print(f"\nImproving description...", file=sys.stderr)
193: 
194:         t0 = time.time()
195:         # Strip test scores from history so improvement model can't see them
196:         blinded_history = [
197:             {k: v for k, v in h.items() if not k.startswith("test_")}
198:             for h in history
199:         ]
200:         new_description = improve_description(
201:             skill_name=name,
202:             skill_content=content,
203:             current_description=current_description,
204:             eval_results=train_results,
205:             history=blinded_history,
206:             model=model,
207:             log_dir=log_dir,
208:             iteration=iteration,
209:         )
210:         improve_elapsed = time.time() - t0
211: 
212:         if verbose:
213:             print(f"Proposed ({improve_elapsed:.1f}s): {new_description}", file=sys.stderr)
214: 
215:         current_description = new_description
216: 
217:     # Bad baselines can be optimized, but cannot win even with perfect scores.
218:     eligible_history = [item for item in history if item["eligible"]]
219:     if not eligible_history:
220:         raise ValueError("No eligible description candidate was evaluated")
221:     # Find the best eligible iteration by TEST score (or train without holdout).
222:     if test_set:
223:         best = max(eligible_history, key=lambda h: h["test_passed"] or 0)
224:         best_score = f"{best['test_passed']}/{best['test_total']}"
225:     else:
226:         best = max(eligible_history, key=lambda h: h["train_passed"])
227:         best_score = f"{best['train_passed']}/{best['train_total']}"
228: 
229:     if verbose:
230:         print(f"\nExit reason: {exit_reason}", file=sys.stderr)
231:         print(f"Best score: {best_score} (iteration {best['iteration']})", file=sys.stderr)
232: 
233:     return {
234:         "exit_reason": exit_reason,
235:         "original_description": original_description,
236:         "best_description": best["description"],
237:         "best_score": best_score,
238:         "best_train_score": f"{best['train_passed']}/{best['train_total']}",
239:         "best_test_score": f"{best['test_passed']}/{best['test_total']}" if test_set else None,
240:         "final_description": current_description,
241:         "iterations_run": len(history),
242:         "holdout": holdout,
243:         "train_size": len(train_set),
244:         "test_size": len(test_set),
245:         "history": history,
246:     }
247: 
248: 
249: def main():
250:     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Run eval + improve loop")
251:     parser.add_argument("--eval-set", required=True, help="Path to eval set JSON file")
252:     parser.add_argument("--skill-path", required=True, help="Path to skill directory")
253:     parser.add_argument("--description", default=None, help="Override starting description")
254:     parser.add_argument("--num-workers", type=int, default=10, help="Number of parallel workers")
255:     parser.add_argument("--timeout", type=int, default=30, help="Timeout per query in seconds")
256:     parser.add_argument("--max-iterations", type=int, default=5, help="Max improvement iterations")
257:     parser.add_argument("--runs-per-query", type=int, default=3, help="Number of runs per query")
258:     parser.add_argument("--trigger-threshold", type=float, default=0.5, help="Trigger rate threshold")
259:     parser.add_argument("--holdout", type=float, default=0.4, help="Fraction of eval set to hold out for testing (0 to disable)")
260:     parser.add_argument("--model", required=True, help="Model for improvement")
261:     parser.add_argument("--verbose", action="store_true", help="Print progress to stderr")
262:     parser.add_argument("--report", default="auto", help="Generate HTML report at this path (default: 'auto' for temp file, 'none' to disable)")
263:     parser.add_argument("--results-dir", default=None, help="Save all outputs (results.json, report.html, log.txt) to a timestamped subdirectory here")
264:     args = parser.parse_args()
265: 
266:     eval_set = json.loads(Path(args.eval_set).read_text())
267:     skill_path = Path(args.skill_path)
268: 
269:     if not (skill_path / "SKILL.md").exists():
270:         print(f"Error: No SKILL.md found at {skill_path}", file=sys.stderr)
271:         sys.exit(1)
272: 
273:     name, _, _ = parse_skill_md(skill_path)
274: 
275:     # Set up live report path
276:     if args.report != "none":
277:         if args.report == "auto":
278:             timestamp = time.strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
279:             live_report_path = Path(tempfile.gettempdir()) / f"skill_description_report_{skill_path.name}_{timestamp}.html"
280:         else:
281:             live_report_path = Path(args.report)
282:         # Open the report immediately so the user can watch
283:         live_report_path.write_text("<html><body><h1>Starting optimization loop...</h1><meta http-equiv='refresh' content='5'></body></html>")
284:         webbrowser.open(str(live_report_path))
285:     else:
286:         live_report_path = None
287: 
288:     # Determine output directory (create before run_loop so logs can be written)
289:     if args.results_dir:
290:         timestamp = time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d_%H%M%S")
291:         results_dir = Path(args.results_dir) / timestamp
292:         results_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
293:     else:
294:         results_dir = None
295: 
296:     log_dir = results_dir / "logs" if results_dir else None
297: 
298:     output = run_loop(
299:         eval_set=eval_set,
300:         skill_path=skill_path,
301:         description_override=args.description,
302:         num_workers=args.num_workers,
303:         timeout=args.timeout,
304:         max_iterations=args.max_iterations,
305:         runs_per_query=args.runs_per_query,
306:         trigger_threshold=args.trigger_threshold,
307:         holdout=args.holdout,
308:         model=args.model,
309:         verbose=args.verbose,
310:         live_report_path=live_report_path,
311:         log_dir=log_dir,
312:     )
313: 
314:     # Save JSON output
315:     json_output = json.dumps(output, indent=2)
316:     print(json_output)
317:     if results_dir:
318:         (results_dir / "results.json").write_text(json_output)
319: 
320:     # Write final HTML report (without auto-refresh)
321:     if live_report_path:
322:         live_report_path.write_text(generate_html(output, auto_refresh=False, skill_name=name))
323:         print(f"\nReport: {live_report_path}", file=sys.stderr)
324: 
325:     if results_dir and live_report_path:
326:         (results_dir / "report.html").write_text(generate_html(output, auto_refresh=False, skill_name=name))
327: 
328:     if results_dir:
329:         print(f"Results saved to: {results_dir}", file=sys.stderr)
330: 
331: 
332: if __name__ == "__main__":
333:     main()

FILE skill-creator-advance/scripts/utils.py
1: """Shared utilities for skill-creator scripts."""
2: 
3: from pathlib import Path
4: 
5: 
6: 
7: def parse_skill_md(skill_path: Path) -> tuple[str, str, str]:
8:     """Parse a SKILL.md file, returning (name, description, full_content)."""
9:     content = (skill_path / "SKILL.md").read_text()
10:     lines = content.split("\n")
11: 
12:     if lines[0].strip() != "---":
13:         raise ValueError("SKILL.md missing frontmatter (no opening ---)")
14: 
15:     end_idx = None
16:     for i, line in enumerate(lines[1:], start=1):
17:         if line.strip() == "---":
18:             end_idx = i
19:             break
20: 
21:     if end_idx is None:
22:         raise ValueError("SKILL.md missing frontmatter (no closing ---)")
23: 
24:     name = ""
25:     description = ""
26:     frontmatter_lines = lines[1:end_idx]
27:     i = 0
28:     while i < len(frontmatter_lines):
29:         line = frontmatter_lines[i]
30:         if line.startswith("name:"):
31:             name = line[len("name:"):].strip().strip('"').strip("'")
32:         elif line.startswith("description:"):
33:             value = line[len("description:"):].strip()
34:             # Handle YAML multiline indicators (>, |, >-, |-)
35:             if value in (">", "|", ">-", "|-"):
36:                 continuation_lines: list[str] = []
37:                 i += 1
38:                 while i < len(frontmatter_lines) and (frontmatter_lines[i].startswith("  ") or frontmatter_lines[i].startswith("\t")):
39:                     continuation_lines.append(frontmatter_lines[i].strip())
40:                     i += 1
41:                 description = " ".join(continuation_lines)
42:                 continue
43:             else:
44:                 description = value.strip('"').strip("'")
45:         i += 1
46: 
47:     return name, description, content

FILE skill-refactor/NOTICE
1: skill-refactor
2: ==============
3: 
4: Original design — not a port
5: -----------------------------
6: 
7: This skill is an **original design** authored for monkey-skills.
8: It is **not** a port, fork, or adaptation of any existing skill.
9: 
10: Although the broader idea of "autonomous skill optimization with
11: git ratchet" was popularized by `alchaincyf/darwin-skill` (which
12: was inspired by Andrej Karpathy's `autoresearch`), the architecture,
13: gate function, evaluation rubric, multi-judge ensemble protocol,
14: refactor moves catalog, and integration with the broader monkey-
15: skills convention library are all independent design.
16: 
17: Specific design distinctions from `darwin-skill`:
18: 
19: 1. **Phase A / Phase B split** — `darwin-skill` mixes structural
20:    refactoring with output quality evaluation in a single 8-dim
21:    rubric. `skill-refactor` deliberately handles only Phase A
22:    (structure with output preservation); Phase B (output quality
23:    A/B) is the separate `skill-dev-toolkit:skill-tuning` skill. This
24:    split avoids the LLM-as-judge / Goodhart drift problems that
25:    monolithic taste-rubrics produce.
26: 2. **Multi-judge ensemble** — `skill-refactor` uses a 3-judge
27:    ensemble with varied prompt framing (utility / content /
28:    boundary) for Q1's semantic equivalence check, rather than a
29:    single judge subagent. Apply the consensus rules to disagreement;
30:    escalate only `FLAG_TIER_2` outcomes to human review.
31: 3. **Specific-behavior-diff override** — a single dissenting judge
32:    citing concrete behavior change outranks numeric majority. This
33:    prevents subtle regressions from sneaking through "2/3 says
34:    equivalent" votes when one judge's specific reason was correct.
35: 4. **Three-question gate (Q1 equivalence + Q2 token reduction ≥10%
36:    + Q3 invariant preservation)** — vs `darwin-skill`'s 8-dim
37:    weighted score. The three-question shape parallels loom-workflow's
38:    critique skills (`proposal-critique`,
39:    `complexity-critique`).
40: 5. **Verdict vocabulary** — PROCEED / RESHAPE / REJECT — parallel
41:    to `complexity-critique`. `darwin-skill` uses keep / revert.
42: 6. **Tier 1 / 2 / 3 cascade** — graceful degradation from
43:    automatic verdict to human escalation to human-judge handoff
44:    (`skill-tuning`). `darwin-skill` does not have this cascade.
45: 7. **Refactor-hat editor prompt explicitly excludes
46:    behavior-changing moves** — Out-of-Scope Moves table in the
47:    refactor-moves catalog enforces Fowler's Two Hats discipline.
48: 8. **Bundled convention files** — `golden-anchor-protocol.md` /
49:    `test-prompts-schema.md` / `constitution-schema.md` ride in this
50:    skill's `references/` for runtime self-containment. They are
51:    shared with `skill-dev-toolkit:skill-tuning` (when shipped) via the
52:    same-PR drift rule, not via cross-skill runtime path delegation.
53: 
54: Inspirations acknowledged
55: -------------------------
56: 
57: The autonomous-loop concept that informs this skill traces:
58: 
59: 1. Andrej Karpathy — `autoresearch` (https://github.com/karpathy/autoresearch)
60:    Original "keep only measurable improvements, revert everything else"
61:    pattern applied to ML research / code optimization.
62: 
63: 2. alchaincyf — `darwin-skill` (MIT,
64:    https://github.com/alchaincyf/darwin-skill)
65:    First published application of the autoresearch pattern to
66:    Claude Agent Skills. Demonstrated that the autonomous-loop +
67:    git ratchet shape works for skill optimization.
68: 
69: 3. Fowler, M. — *Refactoring* (2nd ed, 2018) and the Two Hats
70:    principle. Grounds this skill's discipline of refusing
71:    behavior-changing edits in refactor scope.
72: 
73: 4. monkey-skills' skill-evolution architecture rationale — covers
74:    splitting Phase A / Phase B and the cost-blind H1-H4 horizons that
75:    informed PR-2's scope.
76: 
77: License
78: -------
79: 
80: MIT — see LICENSE in this directory.
81: 
82: Copyright (c) 2026 kouko.
83: 
84: No copyright dependencies on the inspirations above; design
85: borrowing is acknowledged here for transparency, not because the
86: license requires it. (`darwin-skill` is MIT, `autoresearch` is MIT;
87: neither was modified or directly incorporated.)
88: 
89: Convention sharing with skill-tuning
90: --------------------------------------
91: 
92: When `skill-dev-toolkit:skill-tuning` ships (PR-3 of this series), the
93: following 3 convention files will be present as functional copies
94: in both skills' `references/`:
95: 
96: - `golden-anchor-protocol.md`
97: - `test-prompts-schema.md`
98: - `constitution-schema.md`
99: 
100: **Same-PR drift rule**: any edit to
101: the protocol must update both copies in the same PR. This skill
102: ships the canonical content for these conventions in PR-2;
103: `skill-tuning` will copy them verbatim plus a header blockquote
104: pointing back. Drift between the two copies is a regression
105: caught by future cross-skill CI (PR-4 of this series).

FILE skill-refactor/README.ja.md
1: # Skill Refactor
2: 
3: [English](README.md) | **日本語** | [繁體中文](README.zh-TW.md)
4: 
5: > 既存 skill のトークン / 構造リファクタ — 出力動作を保持し、
6: > multi-judge ensemble + 構造化比較で等価性を証明、退化したら
7: > git revert で巻き戻し。
8: 
9: ユーザーが明示的に呼び出す **gate skill**：既存 skill の
10: `SKILL.md` がトークン / cruft を蓄積していて、**skill の動作を
11: 変えずに**短縮 / 整理したいときに呼び出す。出力等価性を hard
12: precondition として強制し、トークン削減編集は等価検証を通って
13: からでないと commit されない。
14: 
15: この README は GitHub で読む人間向け。Claude が実際にロードする
16: operational ファイルは [`SKILL.md`](SKILL.md)。
17: 
18: ---
19: 
20: ## なぜこの skill が存在するのか？
21: 
22: **繰り返される失敗モード**：skill はトークンを蓄積する。SKILL.md
23: は編集を重ねるたびに大きくなる。多くの編集は additive — コーナー
24: ケースを補う、例を加える。結果として：必要以上のトークンを読み込
25: む skill になり、出力動作は以前と同じ（あるいは悪化 — 長い
26: prompt はかえって focus を薄める）。
27: 
28: 明示的な gate がなければ、編集はデフォルトで *additive* になる。
29: この skill は additive default を捕まえる規律を、**特にトークン /
30: 構造作業に絞って**凝縮し、動作変更を scope から除外する。
31: 
32: 規律は 3 つのチェック：
33: 
34: 1. **出力等価性** — 3-judge ensemble + 構造化比較で証明、編集者の
35:    主張ではない
36: 2. **トークン削減 ≥10%** — 化粧的な微調整は ratchet クレジット
37:    を得ない
38: 3. **Invariants の保持** — name / dependencies / contract / ファ
39:    イル構造が不変
40: 
41: 3 つのうちどれかが失敗 → `git revert`。**ratchet は前にしか進ま
42: ない**。
43: 
44: ---
45: 
46: ## どう動くのか？
47: 
48: ### Operational flow の概観
49: 
50: ```mermaid
51: flowchart TD
52:     A([ユーザーが既存 skill X に対し呼び出し]) --> B[Phase 0: baseline 取得]
53:     B --> B1[現 SKILL.md に test-prompts.json を実行]
54:     B --> B2[トークン数 + invariants 記録]
55:     B1 --> C[Phase 1: refactor move 提案]
56:     B2 --> C
57:     C --> D[move 適用 + git commit]
58:     D --> Q1{Q1: 出力等価?}
59:     Q1 -->|FAIL| R[git revert + 失敗記録]
60:     Q1 -->|FLAG| H[Tier 2 人間にエスカレーション]
61:     Q1 -->|PASS| Q2{Q2: トークン削減 ≥10%?}
62:     Q2 -->|≥10%| Q3{Q3: Invariants 完全?}
63:     Q2 -->|5%以上10%未満| WC[RESHAPE: 弱い win, user 判断]
64:     Q2 -->|<5% or 増加| R
65:     Q3 -->|PASS| K[PROCEED — 保持, ratchet 前進]
66:     Q3 -->|FAIL| R
67:     R --> NEXT[次のラウンド / skill]
68:     K --> NEXT
69:     WC --> NEXT
70:     H --> NEXT
71: ```
72: 
73: ### 3 つのチェック
74: 
75: | チェック | メカニズム | 失敗 → |
76: |---|---|---|
77: | **Q1 等価性** | Layer 1 構造（決定論的）+ Layer 2 LLM-judge ensemble（3 コール、フレーム異なり）| REJECT または FLAG (Tier 2) |
78: | **Q2 トークン削減** | `wc -w` 前後；≥10% しきい値 | REJECT（5%以上10%未満 は RESHAPE）|
79: | **Q3 Invariants** | name / dependencies / contract / 構造が不変 | REJECT |
80: 
81: ### Verdict 語彙
82: 
83: loom-workflow の critique skill と並行：
84: 
85: | Verdict | 条件 | アクション |
86: |---|---|---|
87: | **PROCEED** | 3 つ全部厳密に pass | Ratchet 前進、commit 保持 |
88: | **RESHAPE** | Q2 弱い（5%以上10%未満）が Q1+Q3 通過 | user に提示、保持 / 続行を判断 |
89: | **REJECT** | Q1/Q2/Q3 のいずれか失敗 | `git revert`、results.tsv に記録、次へ |
90: | (Tier 2) | Q1 = `FLAG_TIER_2` | 人間 review にエスカレーション |
91: 
92: ### Multi-judge ensemble（核となる innovation）
93: 
94: LLM-as-judge には既知の失敗モード（verbosity bias、position bias、
95: self-preference、微妙な変化への鈍感さ）がある。単一 judge による
96: 等価性判定は信頼性に欠ける。この skill は 3 つの judge を異なる
97: prompt フレーミングで spawn する：
98: 
99: | Judge | Frame | 何を捕まえるか |
100: |---|---|---|
101: | 1 | 「user にとって同じ価値か」（utility）| 構造保持 |
102: | 2 | 「同じ情報集合か」（content）| 暗黙の内容欠落 |
103: | 3 | 「同じエッジケース処理か」（boundary）| 失われた fallback / warning |
104: 
105: 加えて：**specific-behavior-diff override**。単一 judge が
106: *具体的な*動作変更を引用すると、2/3 多数決を上書きする。これが
107: 「リファクタを装った feature work」を捕まえる仕組み。
108: 
109: 完全な protocol：[`references/multi-judge-ensemble.md`](references/multi-judge-ensemble.md)。
110: 
111: ---
112: 
113: ## いつ使うべきか？
114: 
115: ### 以下の場合に呼び出す…
116: 
117: - 既存 skill の SKILL.md が長すぎ / 重複 / cruft があり、整理し
118:   たい
119: - 以下のような言葉を打った：
120:   - 「shorten this skill」
121:   - 「reduce token count」
122:   - 「縮減 SKILL.md」
123:   - 「整理 skill 結構」
124:   - 「動作を変えずに refactor」
125:   - 「リファクタ skill / トークン削減」
126: - リファクタ対象 skill が `test-prompts.json` を持つ（または
127:   作成可能で）≥3 つの代表的 prompt を含む
128: - 出力動作を**明示的に**保持したい
129: 
130: ### 以下の場合は呼び出**さない**…
131: 
132: - **skill 出力が悪い / 間違っている** → `skill-dev-toolkit:skill-tuning`
133:   を使う — 品質改善は人間 A/B 必要、等価保持
134:   リファクタではない
135: - **phase 追加 / agent 変更 / workflow 再構築** →
136:   `skill-dev-toolkit:skill-creator-advance` — 構造的書き直しは feature hat
137: - **新規 skill 作成** → `skill-dev-toolkit:skill-creator-advance`
138: - **1 行の cosmetic 編集** — 直接編集で十分、gate コストが編集
139:   コストを超える
140: - **skill が `test-prompts.json` を持たず user が書きたがらない** —
141:   等価チェックが走れない、prompt を用意するか
142:   `skill-creator-advance` で test infra 含めて再設計
143: - **skill 出力が創造的 / 非決定論的**（文体、散文、デザイン感）—
144:   等価チェック不信頼、`skill-tuning` を使う
145: 
146: ---
147: 
148: ## 出力はどんな形？
149: 
150: ### Worked Example — skill-creator-advance のトークン膨張
151: 
152: **Input**：ユーザー「skill-creator-advance が 5627 語、soft cap
153: を大幅超過。リファクタして」。
154: 
155: **Phase 0（baseline）**：
156: - `test-prompts.json` を取得（3 prompt：作成 / 改善 / description
157:   optimization）
158: - 現 skill に対して baseline 実行 → outputs を `<workspace>/baseline/`
159:   に保存
160: - エントリ語数：5627 語；package mode でパッケージ全体の baseline を記録
161: - Invariant snapshot：`name` / 依存 / 構造を記録
162: 
163: **Phase 1, Round 1**：
164: - Move：Description Optimization セクション（~700 語）を
165:   `references/description-optimization.md` に抽出
166: - Q1 ensemble（仮の結果）：3/3 say 出力等価（description-opt の使用ケースは
167:   reference が on-demand で読まれるので動作する）
168: - Q2：エントリは 5627 → 4927 = 12.4% 縮小。ただし ~700 語は
169:   reference に残る。パッケージ全体の語数は同じか参照リンク分だけ増える。
170:   文章の移動だけでは削減にならない。
171: - Q3：name と既存の依存を保持；等価な抽出に必要な bundled reference の
172:   追加は package mode で許容 ✓
173: - **Verdict: REJECT** — Q1 と Q3 が通っても whole-package Q2 は
174:   不合格のため、この候補を破棄する。
175: 
176: 次の候補では実際の重複を削り、パッケージ全体で Q1/Q2/Q3 を独立に
177: 検証する必要がある。後続ラウンドの検証結果はこの例にはない。
178: 
179: ### Worked Example — REJECT すべき refactor
180: 
181: **Input**：ユーザー「この書き換えを試したい — もっと明確だ」。
182: 
183: - Q1 結果：2/3 judges say 出力等価；1 が反対 — 「candidate の
184:   出力は baseline がやっていた file-permission チェックを skip
185:   している」
186: - Q2 結果：5% 削減
187: - Q3 結果：きれい
188: 
189: 反対 judge の理由は**具体的な動作変更**を引用している。2/3 が
190: 等価と投票しても specific-behavior-diff override が起動する。
191: 
192: **Verdict: FLAG → user が反対意見を review**
193: 
194: User が確認 — はい、その「明確な」書き換えは file-permission
195: チェックを Claude に促していた一節を落とした。リファクタを装った
196: 微妙な動作変更。
197: 
198: → **このラウンドは REJECT、commit しない**。これが multi-judge
199: ensemble が存在する理由。
200: 
201: ---
202: 
203: ## 他の skill との関係は？
204: 
205: この skill は **既存 skill 1 つを動作保持で扱う**。引き渡しは：
206: 
207: - **a proposal-triage gate** — 複数の refactor 提案を
208:   triage して優先順位付け
209: - **a complexity / deletion-first gate** — 問いが「そもそも
210:   refactor すべきか」のとき（smallest-end-state を考えてから
211:   refactor 呼び出し）
212: - **`skill-dev-toolkit:skill-creator-advance`** — 変更が構造的
213:   （phase 追加 / agent 変更 / 再設計）
214: - **`skill-dev-toolkit:skill-tuning`** — 問いが
215:   「出力は等価か」から「どちらの出力が良いか」に変わったとき
216: - **`skill-dev-toolkit:skill-judge`** — オプショナル advisory；
217:   refactor 前 / 後にスコアを取り、等価チェックが通り続けても
218:   スコアが落ちれば微妙な taste drift の信号
219: 
220: ---
221: 
222: ## skill-dev-toolkit の中での位置
223: 
224: skill オーサリングのライフサイクル（すべて `skill-dev-toolkit`）：
225: 
226: - `skill-creator-advance` — 作成 + 再設計
227: - `skill-judge` — advisory 設計スコア
228: - `skill-refactor` — Phase A: トークン / 構造のリファクタ、動作保持
229: - `skill-tuning` — Phase B: 出力 A/B、人間 judge、preference log
230: - `dogfood-skill-testing` — ブラインド挙動テスト
231: 
232: 汎用 critique gate（`proposal-critique` / `complexity-critique`）は
233: `loom-workflow` に残ります。
234: 
235: `skill-refactor`（Phase A）と `skill-tuning`（Phase B）の分割
236: は、Fowler の Two Hats を skill に適用したもの：refactor は動作
237: 保持、tuning は動作変更。**意図的に分離**することで、LLM-as-judge
238: が確実に扱えない rubric-mixing を避ける。
239: 
240: ---
241: 
242: ## Origin / lineage
243: 
244: この skill は **独自設計**、port や fork ではない。
245: 
246: 「自律ループ + git ratchet」のコンセプトは
247: [`alchaincyf/darwin-skill`](https://github.com/alchaincyf/darwin-skill)
248: （MIT）が広めたもので、これ自体は Andrej Karpathy の
249: [`autoresearch`](https://github.com/karpathy/autoresearch) に着想
250: を得ている。
251: 
252: **なぜ独自で派生でないのか**：`darwin-skill` は構造リファクタと
253: 出力品質評価を単一の 8 次元 rubric で混ぜている。この skill
254: （skill-refactor）は意図的に Phase A（構造 + 動作保持）のみを
255: 扱い、Phase B（出力品質 A/B）は別 skill `skill-tuning`。この
256: 分割は単一 rubric が抱える LLM-as-judge / Goodhart drift 問題
257: を回避する。
258: 
259: その他の差異：3-judge ensemble + varied framing（vs 単 judge）、
260: specific-behavior-diff override（vs 多数決）、3 つの具体的問い
261: （vs 8 次元加重スコア）、Tier 1/2/3 cascade（vs バイナリ
262: keep/revert）。
263: 
264: 完全な design-influence 詳細は [`NOTICE`](NOTICE) を参照。
265: 
266: ---
267: 
268: ## 既知の限界
269: 
270: | 限界 | 意味 | 緩和 |
271: |---|---|---|
272: | **test-prompts.json が必要** | ≥3 文書化 test prompt なしでは gate が走らない | skill が self-abort、user に作成を依頼（または skill-creator-advance で test infra 含め再設計）|
273: | **LLM-judge は無謬ではない** | 3-judge ensemble でも微妙な動作変更を見逃す可能性 | Specific-behavior-diff override + Tier 2 人間エスカレーション +（オプショナル）golden anchor 錨定 |
274: | **Token-only metric は粗い** | 30 行の型システムトリックは 100 行のストレートな散文より密度が高いことも | 10% 削減しきい値で tiny-win refactor 防止；実質的 refactor は可視 |
275: | **創造的 skill では「出力保持」が定義困難** | 文体 / 散文 skill には客観的な「等価」がない | 自動 abort、`skill-tuning` を推奨 |
276: | **ラウンド間の累積 drift** | 連続 3 回 moderate-confidence PROCEED が微細な drift を複合 | 連続 3 ラウンド moderate 後、累積 diff を人間 review にフラグ |
277: | **未検証で出荷** | この skill は ≥2 実 skill での dry-run validation 前に出荷 | アーキテクチャ doc §6 の validation gate が OUTSTANDING；PR-2 は PR description にこの caveat 記載 |
278: 
279: ---
